### Agents

**1. TCS AI WisdomNext**
- **Tagline:** A unified GenAI aggregation platform that lets enterprises experiment with, compare, and deploy generative AI solutions from multiple vendors through a single interface.
- **Description:** TCS AI WisdomNext is a platform that aggregates multiple generative AI and cloud services into a unified interface, enabling enterprises to rapidly adopt GenAI at scale with minimal coding. It offers six core capabilities: AI-readiness consulting, pre-configured industry-specific solution blueprints, DevSecOps productivity enhancers, composable archetypes for modular solution creation, intelligent evaluator bots that compare GenAI ecosystems, and an enterprise knowledge house that reduces outcome drift. The platform supports real-time experimentation with GenAI solutions from different vendors, including internal and open-source LLM models, with built-in FinOps spend intelligence and responsible AI governance. Its agentic capabilities support deploying agentic AI, physical AI, and HPC workloads through enterprise AI infrastructure.
- **Use Cases:** Enterprise-wide GenAI solution evaluation and deployment; automated quote generation with real-time inventory and geo-tagging (outdoor advertising); smart mortgage assistant for improved customer experience (banking); application migration and modernization acceleration (insurance).
- **Industries Supported:** Financial Services (BFSI), Manufacturing, Retail & E-commerce, Telecom & Media, Healthcare & Life Sciences, Energy & Utilities, Government & Public Sector
- **Business Functions Supported:** IT Operations, Engineering & DevOps, Data & Analytics, Customer Experience, Operations & Supply Chain
- **Expected Business Outcomes:** Accelerated time-to-market for GenAI solutions through low-code blueprints; cost optimization via native GenAI spend intelligence and centralized governance; improved development productivity across build, deploy, and run phases.
- **Integrations:** AWS, Google Cloud, Microsoft Azure, NVIDIA AI Enterprise, Anthropic, IBM
- **Source:** https://www.tcs.com/what-we-do/services/artificial-intelligence/solution/enterprise-generative-ai-adoption-wisdomnext

**2. TCS MasterCraft (GenAI & Agentic AI Edition)**
- **Tagline:** An agentic AI-powered modernization platform that extracts business logic from legacy code and converts it to modern technologies at 70% lower cost.
- **Description:** TCS MasterCraft is an automation-driven modernization platform enhanced with GenAI and agentic AI capabilities. It transforms legacy applications into modern, cloud-native systems by mining and extracting business logic from outdated code, then converting it to contemporary technologies. The platform includes over 150 specialized solutions for sectors including financial services and supply chain, and uses agentic AI that combines TCS knowledge bases with purposive agents to handle complex modernization programs. It features a centralized design repository, DevSecOps integration for continuous delivery, and human-in-the-loop oversight throughout the transformation process.
- **Use Cases:** Mainframe-to-cloud modernization for banks; COBOL-to-Java migration at scale (50+ million lines); legacy application business logic extraction and documentation; end-to-end DevSecOps pipeline modernization.

- **Business Functions Supported:** Engineering & DevOps, IT Operations, Operations & Supply Chain
- **Expected Business Outcomes:** Over 70% reduction in modernization costs; 2x productivity savings and 3x faster delivery than traditional approaches; 90% effort savings in creating knowledge repositories (per North American bank case study).
- **Integrations:** DevSecOps toolchains, cloud-native platforms (not further specified)
- **Source:** https://www.tcs.com/who-we-are/newsroom/press-release/tcs-launches-genai-agentic-ai-powered-tcs-mastercraft-help-enterprises-swiftly-modernize-legacy-systems

- **Description:** TCS BaNCS AI Compass integrates machine learning, deep learning, generative AI, and pre-built intelligent agents into the existing TCS BaNCS banking and securities platform. It features a no-code, configurable interface that allows teams to quickly build, train, test, and deploy AI agents without writing code or moving data outside secure zones. - **Tagline:** Automatically detects and remediates broken test locators in real time to keep builds green.
- **Description:** The Self-Healing Agent automatically adapts tests during execution when UI elements change, preventing false failures caused by locator breakage. Each time a test interacts with an element, the agent securely stores key information including the locator, nearby attributes, and DOM structure; during future runs, if the original locator no longer resolves, it evaluates historical context and AI signals to generate an alternate healed locator. The agent supports popular automation frameworks including Selenium, Playwright, and Appium for both web and mobile app testing. It is available across BrowserStack Automate, App Automate, and Low-Code Automation products.
- **Use Cases:** Maintaining test suite stability during rapid UI iteration and redesigns; reducing QA maintenance overhead for large-scale automated regression suites; preventing CI/CD pipeline failures caused by benign front-end changes; keeping mobile app test suites functional across frequent app updates.

- **Expected Business Outcomes:** 40% reduction in automation build failures; significant reduction in QA maintenance time.

**4. BrowserStack A11y Issue Detection Agent**
- **Tagline:** Brings human-like intelligence to accessibility testing by detecting issues that traditional automated scanners miss.
- **Description:** The A11y Issue Detection Agent proactively surfaces accessibility compliance gaps by analyzing the DOM, HTML, component structure, accessibility tree, and element behavior against WCAG standards. It uses contextual AI to automatically differentiate between decorative, functional, and images of text, and auto-detects complex images requiring descriptive alt-text. The agent feeds rich context into BrowserStack's Spectra Rule Engine to identify issues such as missing alt text, low color contrast, broken keyboard navigation, and incorrect focus order with human-like precision. It enhances both the depth of coverage and breadth of coverage compared to traditional rule-based scanners.
- **Use Cases:** Automated WCAG compliance auditing across web and mobile applications; detecting and remediating color contrast issues at scale across product portfolios; verifying screen-reader focus order and keyboard navigation accessibility; shifting accessibility testing left into the development workflow for continuous compliance.
- **Industries Supported:** Healthcare & Life Sciences, Financial Services (BFSI), Government & Public Sector, Retail & E-commerce, Education
- **Business Functions Supported:** Engineering & DevOps, Legal & Compliance, IT Operations
- **Expected Business Outcomes:** Elimination of up to 90% of color contrast issues that typically require manual review; improved WCAG compliance coverage.
- **Integrations:** BrowserStack Accessibility Testing, BrowserStack App Accessibility Testing
- **Source:** https://www.browserstack.com/docs/accessibility/automated-tests/ai-powered-testing/issue-detection-agent

**5. BrowserStack Visual Review Agent**
- **Tagline:** Filters noise from visual diffs and helps teams approve meaningful UI changes 3x faster.
- **Description:** The Visual Review Agent streamlines visual testing by using computer vision and machine learning to filter out insignificant pixel-level differences and highlight only meaningful visual changes. It provides human-readable natural-language summaries explaining what changed in each visual diff, and uses bounding boxes to draw attention to significant shifts. The agent includes OCR analysis to avoid flagging minor text or font shifts, and shift detection to isolate changes from new page elements without flagging the entire layout. Teams can create and reuse custom review rules to guide the agent on what to show or ignore, enabling scalable visual review workflows.
- **Use Cases:** Accelerating visual QA review for design system and UI component library updates; automating visual regression detection across cross-browser and cross-device test runs; filtering false-positive visual diffs in CI/CD pipelines to reduce reviewer fatigue; scaling visual testing for large web applications with frequent deployments.
- **Industries Supported:** Healthcare & Life Sciences, Financial Services (BFSI), Retail & E-commerce, Telecom & Media, Education
- **Business Functions Supported:** Engineering & DevOps, IT Operations
- **Expected Business Outcomes:** 3x faster visual change approvals; up to 40% reduction in visual changes requiring manual review.
- **Integrations:** Percy by BrowserStack, BrowserStack Automate
- **Source:** https://www.browserstack.com/percy/ai-agents

Prism supports the full testing lifecycle from data validation through model evaluation and production monitoring, with a low-code interface accessible to both beginners and advanced practitioners.
- **Use Cases:** Automated data quality and model performance testing before AI deployment; detecting data drift, labeling errors, and edge cases in computer vision pipelines; continuous monitoring of AI model behavior in production; validating radiology and medical imaging AI for healthcare compliance
- **Industries Supported:** Healthcare & Life Sciences, Aerospace & Defense, Manufacturing, Financial Services (BFSI)

- **Expected Business Outcomes:** 3x acceleration of AI development cycles; 90% reduction in AI production risks; 3x faster radiology review through validated AI deployment
- **Integrations:** MLOps toolchains, analytics platforms, on-premise deployment support
- **Source:** https://docs.raga.ai/ragaai-prism
